TEXT # Local Issue: local/run#9
REQ-6b PR 评审工作区/评审者/分支保护与合并/关闭重开

State: open
Assignees: @deepseek-9
Parent: Local Issue: local/run#1

## Description

## 交付
REQ-6 的评审与合并控制部分：
- **REQ-6-3-1 详情总览与提交**（requirements.yaml 行 3289–3389）：公开 Open PR 未登录可直接查看（heading=精确标题）；Commits 与 Files changed 是链接；Commits 视图显示 "Commit summary"；Files changed 显示 "Changed files summary"；刷新与离开重开恢复同一 PR；种子含稳定标题、描述、讨论评论、≥1 可比 commit。
- **REQ-6-3-2 变更文件与聚合 diff**（行 3390–3437）：访客从公开 PR 点 "Files changed" 看到逐字文件路径 `src/search.ts` 与聚合文本（格式 “<N> additions, <M> deletions”，如 3 additions, 1 deletions）；种子含一个新增文件+一个修改文件；每个 diff 块显示文件路径与增删行；只读、无权限私有 PR 拿不到 diff。
- **REQ-6-3-3 行内评审评论**（行 3438–3516）：非作者 Write+ 在 Files changed 对变更行激活视觉为 “+”、可访问名 "Add comment" 的按钮（可直接激活、无需先 hover；文档序第一个按钮对应第一个可评论行）；打开唯一编辑器 labeled "Comment"，按钮 "Add single comment"（立即发布，精确显示正文、刷新保留）与 "Start a review"（仅作者可见 "Pending review"，刷新保留草稿、不公开）；存储 PR、文件路径、compare commit、行位置、作者、正文、发布态；compare commit 变化后已发布评论保留并标 Outdated；空评论/失败不产生部分评论；种子：非作者 Write 评审者 bob-reviewer、一个可评审 Open PR、另一个供 pending 场景的 Open PR。
- **REQ-6-3-4 提交评审**（行 3517–3591）："Review changes" 按钮 → 一个表单：可选 "Summary" 字段、radio "Comment"/"Approve"/"Request changes"、按钮 "Submit review"；Approve 无 summary 也有效并显示 "Approved"；Request changes+summary 显示 "Changes requested" 与精确 summary；刷新保留；作者与 Draft PR 不能提交；同一评审者对同一 compare commit 的新决策替换生效决策但保留历史记录；Request changes 阻塞合并直至该评审者改判或 compare 分支新 commit 使其过期。
- **REQ-6-4 请求/移除评审者**（行 3592–3651）：PR 详情右侧 "Reviewers" 按钮打开 picker（textbox "Search"）；输入即出现精确可访问名选项；选择立即保存（无 Save）、关闭 picker、用户名显示在评审者区、刷新保留；每个已请求评审者有按钮 "Remove <username>"（即时移除、无确认、刷新保留移除）；作者（Open/Draft PR）与 Maintain/Admin/org Owner 可操作；候选人须 Write+ 且非作者；移除不影响其已提交的评审/评论/活动；种子：Open PR 属于登录作者、目标为未请求未评审的非作者协作者。
- **REQ-6-5 合并合格 PR**（行 3652–3746）：仅 Maintain/Admin/org Owner；唯一方法 "Create a merge commit"；按钮 "Merge pull request" → 确认区（显示满足/未满足条件）→ "Confirm merge"；合并前重读 base head、当前 compare commit、冲突态与保护规则：无有效 Request changes；保护分支按启用规则独立强制（Require 1 approval ⇒ 1 个当前 compare commit 的非作者有效 Approve；Require status check test ⇒ test success）；非保护分支仍要求无冲突且无有效 Request changes；成功原子创建双亲 merge commit、前移 base head、PR 置 Merged（终态）并记录合并者/时间/结果 commit id；不满足时解释原因（缺审批显示 "Review required by branch protection"）且 base 与 PR 均不变；被阻塞 PR 保持可见但禁用的 "Merge pull request" 按钮并事先解释条件；种子：Maintain 账号、成功与拒绝各一个独立 Open PR。
- **REQ-6-6 不经合并关闭/重开 PR**（行 3747–3819）：按钮 "Close pull request"/"Reopen pull request"；关闭立即生效无确认，重开立即恢复 Open，刷新后 Close 又可用；非作者且非 Maintain/Admin/Owner 的查看者两个控件都不渲染；关闭/重开记录状态+操作者+时间与时间线事件，不更新任何分支；Merged 为终态不显示也不接受状态变更。

## 依赖
- Issue #8（REQ-6a）合入后开工；复用其 PR 详情页框架、种子 PR 与 check 数据。

## 验收
- 自检：详情三标签读取、聚合 diff 文本、行内评论发布/pending/过期标记、评审提交（Approve/Request changes/替换决策）、评审者添加移除、合并成功路径（merge commit 双亲、base 前移）与被阻塞路径（缺审批文案）、关闭重开循环与只读不可见。
- PR `--base develop`。

TEXT // REQ-6-2 pull-request discovery and creation API (Issue #8 / REQ-6a).
//
// Scope of this router:
//   * list + filter read model for the repository PR list (REQ-6-2-1),
//   * read-only branch comparison used before creation (REQ-6-2-2),

//   * the PR detail read model the review workspace (REQ-6-3/6-4/6-5) builds on,
//   * Draft -> Open via "Ready for review" (REQ-6-2-4).
//
// Review submission, merge, close/reopen and reviewer requests belong to

// below already expose the fields those features consume.

    if (!isAuthor && !hasAtLeast(req.repoRole, 'maintain')) {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' });
    }
    if (pr.status !== 'draft') {

      recordActivity(req.repo.id, req.user.id, 'ready_for_review', { number: pr.number, title: pr.title }, at);

// Idempotent seed data, provisioned on every normal start. If the database
// already contains users, seeding is skipped so user-created data survives
// restarts within the same run.
export function seed(db) {
  const count = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM users').get().c;
  if (count > 0) return;

  const now = Date.now();
  const iso = (minutesAgo) => new Date(now - minutesAgo * 60_000).toISOString();

  const tx = db.transaction(() => {
    insertUsers(db, iso);
    const org = insertOrg(db, iso);
    const repos = insertRepos(db, iso);
    seedWebAppContent(db, repos.webApp, iso);
    seedPlaygroundContent(db, repos.playground, iso);
    seedSecretApiContent(db, repos.secretApi, iso);
    seedAcmeDocsContent(db, repos.acmeDocs, iso);
    seedSecretResearchContent(db, repos.secretResearch, iso);
    seedForkConflict(db, repos.acmeDocs, iso);
    insertRepoGrants(db, repos);
    seedIssuesAndPulls(db, repos.webApp, iso);
    insertActivity(db, repos, iso);
    const demoOrg = insertDemoOrg(db, iso);
    seedDemoOrgContent(db, demoOrg, iso);

function insertActivity(db, repos, iso) {
  const alice = user(db, 'alice-dev').id;
  const events = [
    [repos.webApp.id, alice, 'pushed', { branch: 'main', commit: 'Document search flow' }, iso(60 * 24 * 12)],
    [repos.webApp.id, alice, 'opened_issue', { number: 2, title: 'Legacy welcome text' }, iso(60 * 24 * 8)],
  ];
  for (const [repoId, actorId, type, data, at] of events) {
    db.prepare('INSERT INTO activity (repo_id, actor_id, type, data, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)').run(
      repoId,
      actorId,
      type,
      JSON.stringify(data),
      at
    );
  }
}

### 45 L45 2026-09-28T07:31:53.281Z message id=4da7ab46
TEXT import { effectiveRole, getRepo, hasAtLeast, repoOwnerInfo } from './permissions.js';

// Guards an API route by the effective repository role.
// Anonymous or unauthorized access to a private repository is reported as
// 404 to avoid disclosing its existence; signed-in users without the
// required role get 403.
export function repoParamMiddleware(db) {
  return (req, res, next) => {
    const { owner, name } = req.params;
    const repo = getRepo(db, owner, name);
    if (!repo) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Not found' });
    const role = effectiveRole(db, req.user, repo);
    if (!role) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Not found' });
    req.repo = repo;
    req.repoRole = role;
    req.repoOwner = repoOwnerInfo(db, repo);
    next();
  };
}

export function requireRepoRole(minRole) {
  return (req, res, next) => {
    if (!req.repo || !hasAtLeast(req.repoRole, minRole)) {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Forbidden' });
    }
    next();
  };
}

export function requireAuth(req, res, next) {
  if (!req.user) return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Authentication required' });
  next();
}
export const ROLE_LEVELS = { read: 1, triage: 2, write: 3, maintain: 4, admin: 5 };

export function maxRole(a, b) {
  if (!a) return b || null;
  if (!b) return a;
  return ROLE_LEVELS[a] >= ROLE_LEVELS[b] ? a : b;
}

export function getRepo(db, owner, name) {
  return db
    .prepare(
      `SELECT r.* FROM repositories r
       LEFT JOIN organizations o ON r.owner_type = 'org' AND o.id = r.owner_id
       LEFT JOIN users u ON r.owner_type = 'user' AND u.id = r.owner_id
       WHERE r.name = ? AND (
         (r.owner_type = 'org' AND o.name = ?) OR
         (r.owner_type = 'user' AND u.username = ?)
       )`
    )
    .get(name, owner, owner);
}

export function repoOwnerInfo(db, repo) {
  if (repo.owner_type === 'org') {
    const org = db.prepare('SELECT id, name, display_name FROM organizations WHERE id = ?').get(repo.owner_id);
    return { type: 'org', id: org.id, name: org.name, displayName: org.display_name };
  }
  const user = db.prepare('SELECT id, username FROM users WHERE id = ?').get(repo.owner_id);
  return { type: 'user', id: user.id, name: user.username };
}

export function repoOwnerName(db, repo) {
  return repoOwnerInfo(db, repo).name;
}

// Unified effectiveRole(user, repo) computation.
// - Repository owner (user namespace) is Admin.
// - Organization Owner is Admin on every organization repository.
// - Direct grants and team grants are combined; the highest wins.
// - Anonymous visitors get Read on public repositories only.
// - A plain organization Member has NO access to private repositories
//   unless a grant or team grant says otherwise. Organization-level roles
//   other than Owner do not cascade into repository roles.
export function effectiveRole(db, user, repo) {
  if (!user) {
    return repo.visibility === 'public' ? 'read' : null;
  }
  if (repo.owner_type === 'user' && repo.owner_id === user.id) return 'admin';
  if (repo.owner_type === 'org') {
    const owner = db
      .prepare("SELECT 1 FROM org_members WHERE org_id = ? AND user_id = ? AND role = 'owner'")
      .get(repo.owner_id, user.id);
    if (owner) return 'admin';
  }

  let best = null;
  const direct = db
    .prepare("SELECT role FROM grants WHERE repo_id = ? AND subject_type = 'user' AND subject_id = ?")
    .get(repo.id, user.id);
  if (direct) best = maxRole(best, direct.role);

  const teamGrants = db
    .prepare(
      `SELECT g.role FROM grants g
       JOIN teams t ON t.id = g.subject_id
       JOIN team_members tm ON tm.team_id = t.id
       WHERE g.repo_id = ? AND g.subject_type = 'team' AND tm.user_id = ?`
    )
    .all(repo.id, user.id);
  for (const g of teamGrants) best = maxRole(best, g.role);

  if (best) return best;
  return repo.visibility === 'public' ? 'read' : null;
}

export function hasAtLeast(role, min) {
  return !!role && ROLE_LEVELS[role] >= ROLE_LEVELS[min];
}

TEXT export class ApiError extends Error {
  status: number;
  field?: string;
  errors: { error: string; field?: string }[];

  constructor(status: number, body: any) {
    super(body?.error || 'Request failed');
    this.status = status;
    this.field = body?.field;
    this.errors = Array.isArray(body?.errors) && body.errors.length > 0 ? body.errors : (body?.error ? [{ error: body.error, field: body?.field }] : []);
  }
}

export async function api(path: string, options: { method?: string; body?: unknown } = {}) {
  const res = await fetch(`/api${path}`, {
    method: options.method || 'GET',
    headers: options.body !== undefined ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : undefined,
    body: options.body !== undefined ? JSON.stringify(options.body) : undefined,
    credentials: 'same-origin',
  });
  let data: any = null;
  try {
    data = await res.json();
  } catch {
    // non-JSON response
  }
  if (!res.ok) {
    throw new ApiError(res.status, data);
  }
  return data;
}

TEXT import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ApiError, api } from '../api/client';
import type { SettingsResponse } from '../api/types';
import { useApi } from '../api/useApi';
import { RepoNav } from '../components/RepoNav';

// Settings → Branches (REQ-4-3-3): only a repository Admin (or organization
// Owner) sees and can use the "Default branch" select and the "Update" button.
// The branch-protection rules of REQ-6b extend this page.
export function BranchesSettingsPage() {
  const { owner = '', name = '' } = useParams();
  const { data, error, reload } = useApi<SettingsResponse>(`/repos/${owner}/${name}/settings`);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState('');
  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [problem, setProblem] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (data) setSelected(data.defaultBranch);
  }, [data]);

  if (error || !data) {
    return (
      <main className="repo-page">
        <h1>Branches</h1>
        {error ? <p>This repository is not available.</p> : <p>Loading…</p>}
      </main>
    );
  }

  const confirm = async () => {
    setConfirming(false);
    try {
      const result = await api(`/repos/${owner}/${name}/default-branch`, {
        method: 'PUT',
        body: { branch: selected },
      });
      setStatus(`Default branch updated to ${result.defaultBranch}`);
      setProblem(null);
      reload();
    } catch (err) {
      setProblem(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Could not update the default branch');
    }
  };

  return (
    <main className="repo-page">
      <RepoNav repo={data.repo} active="settings" />
      <h1>Branches</h1>
      <nav className="settings-nav" aria-label="Settings">
        <Link to={`/${owner}/${name}/settings/general`}>General</Link>
        <Link to={`/${owner}/${name}/settings/branches`} aria-current="page">
          Branches
        </Link>
      </nav>

      <section aria-label="Default branch">
        <h2>Default branch</h2>
        {data.canAdmin ? (
          <>
            <p className="muted">
              Newly opened repository pages show this branch unless another branch is selected.
            </p>
            <label className="field-label" htmlFor="default-branch">
              Default branch
            </label>
            <select
              id="default-branch"
              name="defaultBranch"
              aria-label="Default branch"
              value={selected}
              onChange={(event) => setSelected(event.target.value)}
            >
              {data.branches.map((branch) => (
                <option key={branch} value={branch}>
                  {branch}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
            <button type="button" className="button-primary" onClick={() => setConfirming(true)}>
              Update
            </button>
          </>
        ) : (
          <p className="muted">
            You do not have permission to change the default branch. The current default branch is{' '}
            <span className="default-branch-name">{data.defaultBranch}</span>.
          </p>
        )}
        {status ? (
          <p className="form-success" role="status">
            {status}
          </p>
        ) : null}
        {problem ? (
          <p className="field-error" role="alert">
            {problem}
          </p>
        ) : null}
      </section>

      {confirming ? (
        <div className="dialog-backdrop" role="presentation">
          <div role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Confirm default branch change" className="dialog">
            <h2>Change default branch</h2>
            <p>
              Change the default branch from{' '}
              <span className="default-branch-name">{data.defaultBranch}</span> to{' '}
              <span className="default-branch-name">{selected}</span>?
            </p>
            <div className="dialog-actions">
              <button type="button" className="button-primary" onClick={() => void confirm()}>
                Confirm
              </button>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => setConfirming(false)}>
                Cancel
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </main>
  );
}

function startServer() {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const child = spawn(process.execPath, ['src/server.js'], {
      cwd: path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, '..'),
      env: process.env,
      stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
    });
    let out = '';
    child.stdout.on('data', (d) => {
      out += d.toString();
      const m = out.match(/listening on http:\/\/([^:]+):(\d+)/);
      if (m) resolve({ child, port: Number(m[2]) });
    });
    child.stderr.on('data', (d) => process.stderr.write(d));
    child.on('exit', (code) => reject(new Error(`server exited early: ${code}`)));
  });
}

function check(name, ok, detail = '') {
  results.push({ name, ok, detail });
  if (!ok) failures += 1;
  console.log(`${ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}  ${name}${ok || !detail ? '' : `\n      -> ${detail}`}`);

async function expectVisible(locator, name, timeout = 8000) {
  try {
    await locator.first().waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout });
    const count = await locator.count();
    if (count > 1) {
      check(name, false, `locator 匹配到 ${count} 个元素（要求唯一）`);
      return false;
    }
    check(name, true);
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    check(name, false, `未出现: ${String(err).split('\n')[0]}`);
    return false;
  }
}

async function expectCount(locator, expected, name) {
  const count = await locator.count();
  check(name, count === expected, `期望 ${expected} 个，实际 ${count} 个`);
}

async function expectHidden(locator, name, timeout = 8000) {
  try {
    await locator.first().waitFor({ state: 'detached', timeout });
    check(name, true);
    return true;
  } catch {
    check(name, false, '仍存在/仍可见');
    return false;
  }
}

async function expectText(locator, expected, name) {
  const text = ((await locator.first().textContent()) || '').trim();
  check(name, text === expected, `期望 "${expected}"，实际 "${text}"`);
}

function gitCommit() {
  const r = spawnSync('git', ['rev-parse', 'HEAD'], { cwd: ROOT, encoding: 'utf8' });
  return (r.stdout || '').trim() || 'unknown';
}

function resolveChromium() {
  const candidates = [
    process.env.CHROMIUM_PATH,
    process.env.CHROME_PATH,
    '/workspace/submission/agent/runtime/bin/chromium',
    '/usr/bin/chromium',
    '/usr/bin/chromium-browser',
    '/usr/bin/google-chrome',
    '/usr/bin/google-chrome-stable',
  ].filter(Boolean);
  for (const candidate of candidates) if (fs.existsSync(candidate)) return candidate;
  return undefined;
}

        creatorId: req.user.id,
      });
      return res.status(201).json({
        branch: { name: created.name, commit: created.commit, shortSha: shortSha(created.commit) },
        defaultBranch: req.repo.default_branch,
        branches: branchNames(req.repo.id),
      });
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.expose) return res.status(err.status).json({ error: err.message, field: err.field });
      throw err;
    }
  });

  // -------------------------------------------- default branch (REQ-4-3-3)
  router.get('/repos/:owner/:name/settings', (req, res) => {
    return res.json({
      repo: repoSummary(req),
      defaultBranch: req.repo.default_branch,
      branches: branchNames(req.repo.id),
      ...repoContext(req),
    });
  });

  router.put('/repos/:owner/:name/default-branch', requireRepoRole('admin'), (req, res) => {
    const { branch } = req.body || {};
    const name = typeof branch === 'string' ? branch.trim() : branch;
    if (!name || !branchRow(db, req.repo.id, name)) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Branch not found', field: 'branch' });
    }
    db.prepare('UPDATE repositories SET default_branch = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?').run(
      name,
      new Date().toISOString(),
      req.repo.id
    );
    return res.json({ defaultBranch: name, branches: branchNames(req.repo.id) });
  });

  // ------------------------------------------------ file commit (REQ-4-4)
  router.post('/repos/:owner/:name/commits', requireRepoRole('write'), (req, res) => {
    const body = req.body || {};
    const branch = typeof body.branch === 'string' ? body.branch.trim() : req.repo.default_branch;
    const target = typeof body.path === 'string' ? body.path : body.fileName;
    const problems = [];
    const branchInfo = branchRow(db, req.repo.id, branch);
    if (!branchInfo) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Branch not found', field: 'branch' });
    }
    if (isBranchProtected(db, req.repo.id, branch)) {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Branch is protected', field: 'branch' });
    }
    const tree = getTree(db, branchInfo.head_commit_id);
    const pathProblem = validateFilePath(typeof target === 'string' ? target.trim() : target, tree);
    if (pathProblem) problems.push({ error: pathProblem, field: 'path' });
    const messageProblem = validateCommitMessage(body.message);
    if (messageProblem) problems.push({ error: messageProblem, field: 'message' });
    if (problems.length > 0) {
      const payload = fieldErrors(problems);
      return res.status(400).json(payload);
    }
    try {
      const created = createFileCommit(db, req.repo, {
        branch,
        authorId: req.user.id,
        path: target,
        previousPath: body.previousPath || null,
        content: body.content === undefined || body.content === null ? '' : String(body.content),
        message: body.message,
      });
      return res.status(201).json({
        commit: { sha: created.id, shortSha: shortSha(created.id), message: String(body.message).trim() },
        branch,
        path: created.path,
        previousPath: created.previousPath,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.expose) return res.status(err.status).json({ error: err.message, field: err.field });
      throw err;
    }
  });

  return router;

[2 more lines in file. Use offset=350 to continue.]

Hmm, but that's scope creep risk. Let me re-read the issue description's REQ-6-5 line: "合并前重读 base head、当前 compare commit、冲突态与保护规则：无有效 Request changes；保护分支按启用规则独立强制（Require 1 approval ⇒ 1 个当前 compare commit 的非作者有效 Approve；Require status check test ⇒ test success）". This requires protection rules to exist. REQ-6-5 seed says "The eligible PR targets protected main, has a current non-author approval and test: success".

So the seed must have `main` protected with both rules, and PRs for success/refusal. And REQ-6-1's acceptance scenarios need the Add branch protection rule UI. I'll implement REQ-6-1 fully but note it in the issue.

Also REQ-6-5 requires conflict detection: "no merge conflicts". Need a way to determine mergeability. We have a tree-based model: merge conflict if both base and compare changed the same file relative to merge base? Let's think. In this simplified git model, each commit has one parent. A "merge commit whose parents are the target-branch head at merge time and the current compare commit". Conflict detection: need to detect if applying compare diff onto base head conflicts. Simple approach: find merge base (common ancestor), compute diff(base_head vs merge_base) and diff(compare vs merge_base); if the same path is modified in both with different resulting content and not identical content in both branches... Actually if compare's version differs from base's version AND compare content differs from merge-base content, then it's a conflict. Standard 3-way merge: if base head changed path from merge base and compare changed path from merge base to different content → conflict.

Let me design a `mergeability` helper in git.js:
- commitAncestors(db, repoId, id) → set/order
- mergeBase(db, repoId, a, b): walk ancestors of b, find first also ancestor of a (or vice versa).
- conflicts(db, repoId, baseHeadId, compareId): mergeBase m; for each path in union of trees(m), trees(baseHead), trees(compare): 
  - baseChanged = baseTree.get(p) !== mTree.get(p)
  - compareChanged = compareTree.get(p) !== mTree.get(p)
  - if baseChanged && compareChanged && baseTree.get(p) !== compareTree.get(p) → conflict.
  Note: if base head is an ancestor of compare (typical PR: base hasn't moved), mergeBase = baseHead, baseChanged = false for all → no conflicts. Good.
 
- merge commit creation: create a commit with two parents. But the `commits` table has a single `parent_id`. Hmm. "atomically creates a merge commit whose parents are the target-branch head at merge time and the current compare commit". The schema only supports parent_id. We need to record second parent. Options: add a `second_parent_id` column to commits via migration, or add a separate table `commit_parents`. Since schema.js is `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS`, adding a column requires ALTER TABLE migration. Let me look at db.js to see migration approach.

### 64 L64 2026-09-28T07:32:21.171Z message id=7d9793e6
TEXT import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { SCHEMA } from './schema.js';

// The data file lives in a temporary directory by default (never in a
// platform-reserved directory). DATA_DIR can override it for tests.
export function initDataDir() {
  if (process.env.DATA_DIR) {
    fs.mkdirSync(process.env.DATA_DIR, { recursive: true });
    return process.env.DATA_DIR;
  }
  return fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'collab-platform-data-'));
}

export function initDb(dataDir) {
  const db = new Database(path.join(dataDir, 'app.db'));
  db.pragma('journal_mode = WAL');
  db.pragma('foreign_keys = ON');
  db.exec(SCHEMA);
  return db;
}

Add a small migration in db.js: check pragma table_info(commits) for merge_parent_id; if absent, ALTER TABLE commits ADD COLUMN merge_parent_id TEXT. That's safe and additive.

   Actually, do I even need second parent stored? The requirement: "atomically creates a merge commit whose parents are the target-branch head at merge time and the current compare commit", and acceptance likely checks the merge commit has two parents. For our own e2e/backend tests we can verify via API `mergeCommit: { parents: [baseHead, compareCommit] }`. Storing is important for evidence. I'll add `merge_parent_id`.

2. Merge endpoint `POST /repos/:owner/:name/pulls/:number/merge`:
   - require maintain+ (via requireRepoRole('maintain')? org Owner = admin, fine).
   - PR must exist, status open (draft/closed/merged rejected). Draft → error; Merged → terminal error.
   - Re-read base branch head (current), compare commit (PR's compare_commit_id), conflicts, protection rules.
   - Conditions checks list with satisfied boolean + message. Compute:
     - not draft, status open

     - noValidRequestChanges: valid request_changes = review by non-author? Actually "any valid Request changes blocks merging". A review is valid for the current compare commit (compare_commit_id === pr.compare_commit_id) and from a non-author reviewer. The author can't review (per 6-3-4), so just require no current request_changes review.
     - If protected: require_1_approval → ≥1 valid approve by non-author reviewer on current compare commit; require_status_check_test → test check success for current compare commit.
   - If all satisfied: create merge commit with tree = compare tree (should merge content; since conflict-free we can just take compare tree, which includes base changes? Not necessarily — compare branch may not include base's new commits. We need the merged tree = result of applying compare diff onto base head. With conflict detection based on 3-way, we can compute merged tree: start from base head tree, apply compare changes relative to merge base for each path where compare changed and (base didn't change or same). Specifically for each path: if compareChanged: mergedTree.set(p, compareTree.get(p) ?? delete) else keep baseTree. For conflicts, we reject. This is a standard fast-forward-ish 3-way merge.
     - Update base branch head to merge commit id.

Activity record with data {number, merger, at, commit} satisfies storage, plus column is cleaner for detail payload.      - Atomic transaction.

3. Close/reopen:

function buildFrontend() {
  if (process.env.SKIP_BUILD === '1' && fs.existsSync(path.join(ROOT, 'frontend/dist/index.html'))) {
    console.log('== 复用已有 frontend/dist（SKIP_BUILD=1）==');
    return;
  }
  console.log('== 构建前端 ==');
  const r = spawnSync('npm', ['run', 'build'], { cwd: path.join(ROOT, 'frontend'), stdio: 'inherit', env: process.env });
  if (r.status !== 0) throw new Error('frontend build failed');
}

      // not up yet
    }
    await new Prom

  console.log(`运行条件    : ${BASE}，DATA_DIR=${DATA_DIR}，chromium=${resolveChromium() || 'playwright 自带'}`);
  console.log(`创建的 PR   : ${createdNumbers.map((c) => `#${c.number} ${c.title}`).join('; ') || '无'}`);

  if (failures > 0) {
    console.log('失败项:');
    for (const r of results.filter((r) => !r.ok)) console.log(` - ${r.name} ${r.detail}`);
    process.exitCode = 1;
  }
}

function sha256(file) {
  try {
    return createHash('sha256').update(fs.readFileSync(file)).digest('hex').slice(0, 16);
  } catch {
    return 'n/a';
  }
}

- Atomic transaction.

### 96 L2 2026-09-28T07:34:17.138Z message id=0eeefd9a
TEXT import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { api } from './client';

// Small data-fetching helper used by the code/branch/file pages: keeps the
// last successful payload while a new branch/path request is in flight.
export function useApi<T>(path: string | null) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<T | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<unknown>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(!!path);
  const [nonce, setNonce] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!path) {
      setData(null);
      setError(null);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    let alive = true;
    setLoading(true);
    api(path)
      .then((d) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        setData(d as T);
        setError(null);
      })
      .catch((e) => {
        if (!alive) return;
        setError(e);
        setData(null);
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (alive) setLoading(false);
      });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [path, nonce]);

  const reload = useCallback(() => setNonce((n) => n + 1), []);
  return { data, error, loading, reload };
}

import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { StatusBadge } from './StatusBadge';
import { buildQuery } from '../utils/repo';

export interface RepoInfo {
  owner: { name: string; type?: string; displayName?: string };
  name: string;
  fullName: string;
  description?: string;
  visibility: string;
  defaultBranch?: string;
}

interface Props {
  repo: RepoInfo;
  active?: 'code' | 'issues' | 'pulls' | 'settings' | null;
  branch?: string;
  // The repository search page must not expose a second link named "Code"
  // (REQ-4-2-3): its result-type link is the only one on the active page.
  showCode?: boolean;
  // Only the repository overview/Code page uses the repository name as the
  // page heading; elsewhere the page keeps a single h1 of its own.
  repoHeading?: boolean;
}

// Shared repository header + navigation used by the code, search and settings
// pages.
export function RepoNav({ repo, active, branch, showCode = true, repoHeading = false }: Props) {
  const codeTarget = `/${repo.fullName}${buildQuery({ ref: branch })}`;
  return (
    <>
      <div className="repo-heading">
        {repoHeading ? (
          <h1>
            <span className="repo-owner">{repo.owner.name}</span>
            <span className="repo-sep">/</span>
            <span className="repo-name">{repo.name}</span>
          </h1>
        ) : (
          <p className="repo-heading-name">
            <span className="repo-owner">{repo.owner.name}</span>
            <span className="repo-sep">/</span>
            <span className="repo-name">{repo.name}</span>
          </p>
        )}
        <StatusBadge status={repo.visibility} />
      </div>
      {repo.description ? <p className="repo-description">{repo.description}</p> : null}
      <nav className="repo-nav" aria-label="Repository">
        {showCode && (
          <Link to={codeTarget} aria-current={active === 'code' ? 'page' : undefined}>
            Code
          </Link>
        )}
        <Link to={`/${repo.fullName}/issues`} aria-current={active === 'issues' ? 'page' : undefined}>
          Issues
        </Link>
        <Link to={`/${repo.fullName}/pulls`} aria-current={active === 'pulls' ? 'page' : undefined}>
          Pull requests
        </Link>
        <Link to={`/${repo.fullName}/settings`} aria-current={active === 'settings' ? 'page' : undefined}>
          Settings
        </Link>
      </nav>
    </>
  );
}

import './Form.css';

interface FieldProps {
  label: string;
  name: string;
  type?: 'text' | 'email' | 'password' | 'checkbox';
  value?: string;
  checked?: boolean;
  onChange: (value: string | boolean) => void;
  error?: string;
  autoComplete?: string;
}

// Labeled form field that shows the field-level error message returned by
// the shared API contract.
export function FormField({ label, name, type = 'text', value, checked, onChange, error, autoComplete }: FieldProps) {
  const id = `field-${name}`;
  if (type === 'checkbox') {
    return (
      <div className="form-field">
        <input
          id={id}
          name={name}
          type="checkbox"
          checked={!!checked}
          onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.checked)}
        />
        <label htmlFor={id}>{label}</label>
        {error && (
          <p className="field-error" role="alert">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div className="form-field">
      <label htmlFor={id}>{label}</label>
      <input
        id={id}
        name={name}
        type={type}
        value={value ?? ''}
        autoComplete={autoComplete}
        aria-invalid={error ? true : undefined}
        aria-describedby={error ? `${id}-error` : undefined}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
      />
      {error && (
        <p className="field-error" id={`${id}-error`} role="alert">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

```js
// ------------------------------------------------------- merge analysis ----

export function ancestorSet(db, repoId, headCommitId) {
  const set = new Set();
  let current = headCommitId;
  while (current && !set.has(current)) {
    set.add(current);
    const c = commitRow(db, current);
    if (!c || c.repo_id !== repoId) break;
    current = c.parent_id;
  }
  return set;
}

export function mergeBaseCommit(db, repoId, a, b) {
  if (!a || !b) return null;
  const ancestorsOfA = new Set();
  let current = a;
  while (current && !ancestorsOfA.has(current)) {
    ancestorsOfA.add(current);
    const c = commitRow(db, current);
    if (!c || c.repo_id !== repoId) break;
    current = c.parent_id;
  }
  current = b;
  const seen = new Set();
  while (current && !seen.has(current)) {
    seen.add(current);
    if (ancestorsOfA.has(current)) return current;
    const c = commitRow(db, current);
    if (!c || c.repo_id !== repoId) break;
    current = c.parent_id;
  }
  return null;
}

// Three-way analysis of merging `compareCommitId` into `baseHeadId`.
export function analyzeMerge(db, repoId, baseHeadId, compareCommitId) {
  const baseTree = getTree(db, baseHeadId);
  const compareTree = getTree(db, compareCommitId);
  const base = mergeBaseCommit(db, repoId, baseHeadId, compareCommitId);
  const baseCommonTree = getTree(db, base);   // empty when no common ancestor
  const conflicts = [];
  const merged = new Map(baseTree);
  const paths = new Set([...baseCommonTree.keys(), ...baseTree.keys(), ...compareTree.keys()]);
  for (const path of [...paths].sort()) {
    const common = baseCommonTree.has(path) ? baseCommonTree.get(path) : null;
    const ours = baseTree.has(path) ? baseTree.get(path) : null;
    const theirs = compareTree.has(path) ? compareTree.get(path) : null;
    const oursChanged = ours !== common;
    const theirsChanged = theirs !== common;
    if (!theirsChanged) continue;
    if (oursChanged && ours !== theirs) { conflicts.push(path); continue; }
    if (theirs === null) merged.delete(path); else merged.set(path, theirs);
  }
  return { clean: conflicts.length === 0, conflicts, merged, base };
}
```

Edge: if baseHeadId === compareCommitId → everything same → clean, merged = baseTree.

TEXT 24:export function newCommitId() {
import { newSessionId } from './passwords.js';

const SESSION_TTL_MS = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
export const SESSION_COOKIE = 'sid';

export function parseCookies(header) {
  const out = {};
  if (!header) return out;
  for (const part of header.split(';')) {
    const idx = part.indexOf('=');
    if (idx === -1) continue;
    const key = part.slice(0, idx).trim();
    const val = part.slice(idx + 1).trim();
    if (key) out[key] = decodeURIComponent(val);
  }
  return out;
}

export function createSession(db, userId) {
  const id = newSessionId();
  const now = new Date();
  const expires = new Date(now.getTime() + SESSION_TTL_MS);
  db.prepare('INSERT INTO sessions (id, user_id, created_at, expires_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)').run(
    id,
    userId,
    now.toISOString(),
    expires.toISOString()
  );
  return id;
}

export function destroySession(db, sessionId) {
  db.prepare('DELETE FROM sessions WHERE id = ?').run(sessionId);
}

```js
function addColumnIfMissing(db, table, column, definition) {
  const cols = db.prepare(`PRAGMA table_info(${table})`).all();
  if (!cols.some((c) => c.name === column)) {
    db.exec(`ALTER TABLE ${table} ADD COLUMN ${column} ${definition}`);
  }
}
export function initDb(dataDir) {
  ...
  db.exec(SCHEMA);
  migrate(db);
  return db;
}
```

```js
const branchProtectionFor = (repoId, branchName) =>
  db.prepare('SELECT * FROM branch_protections WHERE repo_id = ? AND branch_name = ?').get(repoId, branchName) || null;

const validReviewDecision = (pr, compareCommitId, status) => {
  // effective decision per reviewer current for compare commit
  const latest = new Map();
  for (const r of db.prepare('SELECT * FROM pr_reviews WHERE pr_id = ? ORDER BY created_at, id').all(pr.id)) {
    latest.set(r.reviewer_id, r);
  }
  return [...latest.values()].filter((r) => r.status === status && r.compare_commit_id === compareCommitId && r.reviewer_id !== pr.author_id);
};
```

Merge conditions:
```js
const mergeState = (req, pr, baseCommitId, compareCommitId) => {
  const canMaintain = hasAtLeast(req.repoRole, 'maintain');
  const protection = branchProtectionFor(pr.repo_id, pr.target_branch);
  const analysis = (pr.status === 'open' && baseCommitId && compareCommitId) ? analyzeMerge(db, pr.repo_id, baseCommitId, compareCommitId) : { clean: true, conflicts: [] };
  const approvals = validReviewDecision(pr, compareCommitId, 'approve');
  const requestChanges = validReviewDecision(pr, compareCommitId, 'request_changes');
  const test = testCheck(pr, compareCommitId);
  const requireApproval = !!(protection && protection.require_1_approval);
  const requireCheck = !!(protection && protection.require_status_check_test);
  const conditions = [];
  conditions.push({ key:'open', label:'Pull request is open and not a draft', satisfied: pr.status === 'open' });
  conditions.push({ key:'conflicts', label:'No merge conflicts', satisfied: analysis.clean, reason: analysis.conflicts.length ? `This branch has conflicts that must be resolved (${analysis.conflicts.length} file${...})` : null });
  conditions.push({ key:'request_changes', label:'No changes requested', satisfied: requestChanges.length === 0, reason: requestChanges.length ? `${requestChanges.map(r=>usernameOf(r.reviewer_id)).join(', ')} requested changes` : null });
  if (requireApproval) conditions.push({ key:'approval', label:'1 approval from a non-author reviewer', satisfied: approvals.length >= 1, reason: approvals.length ? null : 'Review required by branch protection' });
  if (requireCheck) conditions.push({ key:'status_check', label:'Required status check "test" is successful', satisfied: !!test && test.status === 'success', reason: !test || test.status !== 'success' ? `Required status check "test" is ${test ? test.status : 'pending'}` : null });
  const blocked = conditions.some(c => !c.satisfied);
  return { method:'merge_commit', methodLabel:'Create a merge commit', protected: !!protection, requireOneApproval: requireApproval, requireStatusCheckTest: requireCheck, conditions, blocked, reasons: conditions.filter(c=>!c.satisfied).map(c=>c.reason || c.label), approvedBy: approvals.map(r=>usernameOf(r.reviewer_id)), changesRequestedBy: requestChanges.map(r=>usernameOf(r.reviewer_id)), mergeCommit: pr.merge_commit_id || null, mergedAt: pr.merged_at || null, mergedBy: usernameOf(pr.merged_by_id) };
};
```

Now prDetail's `canMerge` semantics: keep `pr.status === 'open' && canMaintain` for compatibility, and add `merge.blocked`.

Also `canReview`: `pr.status === 'open' && hasAtLeast(req.repoRole,'write') && req.user && req.user.id !== pr.author_id`.
`canComment`: same as canReview but allow open/draft: `['open','draft'].includes(pr.status) && write+ && non-author`.
`canRequestReviewers`: `(req.user && (req.user.id === pr.author_id || maintain+)) && ['open','draft'].includes(pr.status)`.
`canClose`: `(author || maintain+) && ['open','draft'].includes(pr.status)`.
`canReopen`: `(author || maintain+) && pr.status === 'closed'`.

Also `canAdmin` for checks update: hasAtLeast(role,'admin').

Also `canUpdateCheck`: admin && pr.status open? Checks area exists on PR detail; requirement says admin can update in Checks area. Allow admin for any non-merged PR? Just admin.

```js
router.post('/repos/:owner/:name/pulls/:number/comments', requireRepoRole('write'), (req,res) => {
  pr; if (!pr) 404;
  if (pr.status === 'merged' || pr.status === 'closed') 400;
  if (req.user.id === pr.author_id) 403 'The pull request author cannot comment on changed lines'? 
  const body = trimmed(req.body.body);
  if (!body) 400 { error:'Comment cannot be empty', field:'body' };
  const filePath = trimmed(body.filePath);
  const line = Number(body.line);
  const side = body.side === 'old' ? 'old' : 'new';
  const state = body.state === 'pending' ? 'pending' : 'published';
  const { compare } = currentCommitIds(pr);
  const diff = diffFor(base, compare);
  const file = diff.files.find(f => f.path === filePath);
  if (!file) 400 { error:'File is not part of this pull request', field:'filePath' };
  const op = file.ops.find(o => (side==='old' ? o.oldLine : o.newLine) === line && o.type !== 'context');
  if (!op) 400 { error:'Line is not a changed line in this pull request', field:'line' };
  insert; return 201 prDetail
});
```

```js
router.post('/repos/:owner/:name/pulls/:number/merge', requireRepoRole('maintain'), (req,res) => {
  const pr ...;
  if (pr.status === 'merged') return res.status(409).json({ error:'This pull request is already merged', merge: mergeState(...) });
  if (pr.status === 'draft') return res.status(409).json({ error:'Draft pull requests cannot be merged', ... });
  if (pr.status !== 'open') return res.status(409).json({ error:'This pull request is not open', ... });
  const { base, compare } = currentCommitIds(pr);
  const state = mergeState(req, pr, base, compare);
  if (state.blocked) {
    const reason = state.conditions.find(c=>!c.satisfied && c.key==='approval') ? 'Review required by branch protection' : (state.reasons[0] || 'This pull request cannot be merged');
    return res.status(409).json({ error: reason, merge: state });
  }
  const analysis = analyzeMerge(db, pr.repo_id, base, compare);
  const at = nowIso();
  const mergeCommitId = newCommitId();
  const message = `Merge pull request #${pr.number} from ${pr.source_branch} into ${pr.target_branch}`;
  const tx = db.transaction(() => {
    db.prepare('INSERT INTO commits (id, repo_id, parent_id, merge_parent_id, author_id, message, created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)').run(mergeCommitId, pr.repo_id, base, compare, req.user.id, message, at);
    const insert = db.prepare('INSERT INTO commit_trees (commit_id, path, content) VALUES (?,?,?)');
    for (const [p,c] of analysis.merged) insert.run(mergeCommitId, p, c);
    db.prepare('UPDATE branches SET head_commit_id = ? WHERE repo_id = ? AND name = ?').run(mergeCommitId, pr.repo_id, pr.target_branch);
    db.prepare("UPDATE pull_requests SET status='merged', updated_at=?, merged_at=?, merged_by_id=?, merge_commit_id=? WHERE id=?").run(at, at, req.user.id, mergeCommitId, pr.id);
    recordActivity(repo, user, 'merged_pr', {number, title, base: target, compare: source, mergeCommit: mergeCommitId, mergeCommitShort: shortSha}, at);
    db.prepare('UPDATE repositories SET updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?').run(at, pr.repo_id);
  });
  tx();
  return res.json({ merged: true, mergeCommit: {...}, pull: prDetail(...) });
});
```

    const tx = db.transaction(() => {

```js
function seedReviewWorkflow(db, iso) {
```

// ----------------------------------------------------------------- detail --

export type PullTab = 'conversation' | 'commits' | 'files' | 'checks';

export function PullDetailPage({ tab = 'conversation' }: { tab?: PullTab }) {
  const { owner, name } = useRepoParams();
  const { number = '' } = useParams();
  const [pr, setPr] = useState<PullDetail | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<unknown>(null);
  const [actionError, setActionError] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const load = useCallback(() => {
    let alive = true;
    api(`/repos/${owner}/${name}/pulls/${number}`)
      .then((d) => alive && setPr(d))
      .catch((e) => alive && setError(e));
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [owner, name, number]);

  useEffect(() => load(), [load]);

  const readyForReview = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    setActionError(undefined);
    try {
      const updated = await api(`/repos/${owner}/${name}/pulls/${number}/ready-for-review`, { method: 'POST' });
      setPr(updated);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setActionError(err?.message || 'Could not update the pull request');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  if (error) {
    return (
      <main className="repo-page">
        <h1>Not found</h1>
        <p>This pull request does not exist, or you do not have access to it.</p>
      </main>
    );
  }
  if (!pr) {
    return (
      <main className="repo-page">
        <p>Loading…</p>
      </main>
    );
  }

  const baseUrl = `/${owner}/${name}/pulls/${pr.number}`;
  const tabs: { key: PullTab; label: string; to: string }[] = [
    { key: 'conversation', label: 'Conversation', to: baseUrl },
    { key: 'commits', label: 'Commits', to: `${baseUrl}/commits` },
    { key: 'files', label: 'Files changed', to: `${baseUrl}/files` },
    { key: 'checks', label: 'Checks', to: `${baseUrl}/checks` },
  ];

  return (
    <main className="repo-page pull-page">
      <RepoNav repo={pr.repo} active="pulls" />
      <h1 className="pull-title">{pr.title}</h1>
      <div className="pull-subtitle">
        <span className="pull-number">#{pr.number}</span>
        <StatusBadge status={pr.status} />
        <span className="muted">
          <span className="branch-name">{pr.sourceBranch}</span> → <span className="branch-name">{pr.targetBranch}</span>{' '}
          · opened by {pr.author} · {pr.relative}
        </span>
      </div>
      <nav className="pull-tabs" aria-label="Pull request views">
        {tabs.map((t) => (
          <Link key={t.key} to={t.to} aria-current={tab === t.key ? 'page' : undefined}>
            {t.label}
          </Link>
        ))}
      </nav>

      {actionError ? (
        <p className="field-error" role="alert">
          {actionError}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      <div className="pull-layout">
        <div className="pull-main">
          {tab === 'conversation' ? <ConversationTab pr={pr} /> : null}
          {tab === 'commits' ? <CommitsTab pr={pr} owner={owner} name={name} /> : null}
          {tab === 'files' ? <FilesTab pr={pr} onChanged={setPr} /> : null}
          {tab === 'checks' ? <ChecksTab pr={pr} onChanged={setPr} /> : null}
        </div>
        <aside className="pull-sidebar" aria-label="Pull request controls">
          <ReviewersPanel pr={pr} owner={owner} name={name} onChange={setPr} onError={setActionError} />
          <ReviewsPanel pr={pr} />
          <MergePanel pr={pr} owner={owner} name={name} onChange={setPr} onError={setActionError} />
        </aside>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

function CommitsTab({ pr, owner, name }: { pr: PullDetail; owner: string; name: string }) {
  return (
    <section className="pull-commits" aria-label="Commits">
      <h2>Commit summary</h2>
      <p className="muted">
        {plural(pr.commits.length, 'commit')} on <span className="branch-name">{pr.sourceBranch}</span> compared to{' '}
        <span className="branch-name">{pr.targetBranch}</span>
      </p>
      <ul className="commit-list">
        {pr.commits.map((c) => (
          <li key={c.sha}>
            <code>{c.shortSha}</code>{' '}
            <Link to={`/${owner}/${name}/commit/${c.sha}`}>{c.message}</Link>{' '}
            <span className="muted">
              — {c.author}, {c.relative}
            </span>
          </li>
        ))}
        {pr.commits.length === 0 ? <li className="muted">No comparable commits.</li> : null}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}

function FilesTab({ pr }: { pr: PullDetail }) {
  return (
    <section className="pull-files" aria-label="Files changed">
      <h2>Changed files summary</h2>
      <p className="diff-summary">
        {plural(pr.stats.files, 'changed file')} with{' '}
        <span className="additions">{pr.stats.additions} additions</span>,{' '}
        <span className="deletions">{pr.stats.deletions} deletions</span>
      </p>
      {pr.files.length === 0 ? <p className="muted">No files changed.</p> : null}
      {pr.files.map((file) => (
        <section className="diff-file" id={`diff-${file.path}`} key={file.path} aria-label={file.path}>
          <h3 className="diff-file-header">
            <span className="full-path file-path">{file.path}</span>{' '}
            <span className="muted">
              {file.status} <span className="additions">+{file.additions}</span>{' '}
              <span className="deletions">−{file.deletions}</span>
            </span>
          </h3>
          <table className="diff-table">
            <tbody>
              {file.ops.map((op, index) => (
                <tr key={index} className={`diff-row ${op.type}`}>
                  <td className="line-number">{op.oldLine ?? ''}</td>
                  <td className="line-number">{op.newLine ?? ''}</td>
                  <td className="line-marker">{op.type === 'add' ? '+' : op.type === 'delete' ? '-' : ' '}</td>
                  <td className="line-content">{op.line}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </section>
      ))}
    </section>
  );
}

function ChecksTab({ pr }: { pr: PullDetail }) {
  return (
    <section className="pull-checks" aria-label="Checks">
      <h2>Checks</h2>
      <p className="muted">
        Status for commit <code>{pr.compareCommit.shortSha}</code>
      </p>
      <ul className="check-list">
        {pr.checks.map((c) => (
          <li key={c.name}>
            <span className="check-name">{c.name}</span>: <StatusBadge status={c.status} />{' '}
            {c.setter ? <span className="muted">by {c.setter}</span> : null}
          </li>
        ))}
        {pr.checks.length === 0 ? <li className="muted">No checks reported for this commit.</li> : null}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}

```tsx
function FilesTab({ pr, onChanged }: { pr: PullDetail; onChanged: (pr: PullDetail) => void }) {
  const [editor, setEditor] = useState<{ filePath: string; line: number; side: string } | null>(null);
  const [body, setBody] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined);
  const [reviewOpen, setReviewOpen] = useState(false);

  const openEditor = (filePath, line, side) => { setEditor({filePath,line,side}); setBody(''); setError(undefined); };

  const submitComment = async (state: 'published'|'pending') => {
    if (!editor) return;
    if (!body.trim()) { setError('Comment cannot be empty'); return; }
    setBusy(true); setError(undefined);
    try {
      const updated = await api(`/repos/${pr.repo.fullName}/pulls/${pr.number}/comments`, { method:'POST', body: { body, filePath: editor.filePath, line: editor.line, side: editor.side, state } });
      onChanged(updated); setEditor(null); setBody('');
    } catch (err:any) { setError(err?.message || 'Could not add the comment'); }
    finally { setBusy(false); }
  };

}
```

```tsx
function ChecksTab({ pr, onChanged }) {

  );
}
```

export interface RepoSummary {
  owner: { name: string; type?: string; displayName?: string };
  ownerType?: string;
  ownerName?: string;
  name: string;
  fullName: string;
  description: string;
  visibility: string;
  defaultBranch: string;
  isFork?: boolean;
  forkSource?: { owner: string; name: string; fullName: string } | null;
  cloneUrls?: { https: string; ssh: string };
  createdAt?: string;
  updatedAt?: string;
}

export interface ChangedFile {
  path: string;
  name?: string;
  status: string;
  additions?: number;
  deletions?: number;
}

export interface CommitSummary {
  sha: string;
  shortSha: string;
  message: string;
  author: string;
  createdAt: string;
  relative: string;
  parent: string | null;
  parentShort: string | null;
  changedFiles: ChangedFile[];
}

export interface Entry {
  name: string;
  path: string;
  type: string;
  size?: number;
  lines?: number;
}

export interface TreeResponse {
  repo: RepoSummary;
  branch: string;
  defaultBranch: string;
  branches: string[];
  path: string;
  breadcrumbs: { name: string; path: string }[];
  entries: Entry[];
  readme: { path: string; content: string } | null;
  lastCommit: CommitSummary | null;
  commitCount: number;
  isProtected: boolean;
  canWrite: boolean;
  canAdmin: boolean;
  effectiveRole: string;
}

  branches: string[];
  path: string;
  name: string;
  content: string;
  size: number;
  lines: number;
  language: string;
  lastCommit: CommitSummary | null;
  commitCount: number;
  isProtected: boolean;
  canWrite: boolean;
  canAdmin: boolean;
  effectiveRole: string;
}

export interface CommitsResponse {

  branch: string;
  defaultBranch: string;
  branches: string[];

  commits: CommitSummary[];
  canWrite: boolean;
  canAdmin: boolean;
  effectiveRole: string;
}

  oldLine: number | null;
  newLine: number | null;
}

export interface DiffFile {
  path: string;
  name: string;
  status: string;
  additions: number;
  deletions: number;
  ops: DiffOp[];
}

export interface CommitDiffResponse {
  repo: RepoSummary;

  shortSha: string;
  message: string;
  author: string;
  createdAt: string;
  relative: string;
  parent: { sha: string; shortSha: string } | null;
  base: { sha: string; shortSha: string } | null;
  compare: { sha: string; shortSha: string };
  parents: number;

  files: DiffFile[];

  effectiveRole: string;
}

async function signIn(username) {
  const r = await call('POST', '/api/auth/login', { login: username, password: 'Valid-password-123!' }, username);
  assert.equal(r.status, 200, `login ${username}`);
  return r;
}

// Open a second connection to the same SQLite file to provision a protected
// branch precondition (the branch-protection write surface belongs to REQ-6b).
function withDb(fn) {
  const db = new Database(path.join(DATA_DIR, 'app.db'));
  db.pragma('busy_timeout = 5000');
  db.pragma('foreign_keys = ON');
  try {
    return fn(db);
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}

test.after(() => {
  child.kill();
  fs.rmSync(DATA_DIR, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

async function hasActivity(number, type) {
  const r = await withDb((db) =>
    db
      .prepare(
        `SELECT COUNT(*) AS c FROM activity
         WHERE json_extract(data, '$.number') = ? AND type = ?`
      )
      .get(number, type)
  )();
  return r.c > 0;
}

"""REQ-3 end-to-end acceptance: repository search, creation, fork, clone value

The script builds the frontend when needed, starts the real backend on a free

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PASSWORD = "Valid-password-123!"
failures: list[str] = []
checks = 0


def step(name: str) -> None:
    global checks
    checks += 1
    print(f"  [{checks:02d}] {name}", flush=True)


def check(name: str, condition: bool, detail: str = "") -> None:
    if condition:
        print(f"       PASS  {name}", flush=True)
    else:
        failures.append(name)
        print(f"       FAIL  {name} {detail}", flush=True)


def free_port() -> int:
    with socket.socket() as s:
        s.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
        return s.getsockname()[1]


def build_frontend() -> None:
    if not (ROOT / "frontend" / "dist" / "index.html").exists():
        print("building frontend/dist ...", flush=True)
        subprocess.run(["npm", "run", "build"], cwd=ROOT / "frontend", check=True)


def start_server(data_dir: str, port: int) -> subprocess.Popen:
    env = dict(os.environ, DATA_DIR=data_dir, PORT=str(port), HOST="127.0.0.1")
    proc = subprocess.Popen(
        ["node", "src/server.js"],
        cwd=ROOT / "backend",
        env=env,
        stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
        stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,

### REQ-6-3-1 详情总览与提交
公开 Open PR 未登录可直接查看（h1=精确标题）；Conversation/Commits/Files changed 均为链接；Commits 显示 `Commit summary`，Files changed 显示 `Changed files summary`；刷新恢复同一 PR。种子 `Fix search` 含标题、描述、讨论评论与 1 个可比 commit。

### REQ-6-3-2 变更文件与聚合 diff
Files changed 逐字显示 `src/search.ts`（唯一文本节点）与 `main-only.md`，聚合文本 `3 additions, 1 deletions`；每个 diff 块显示文件路径与增删行；只读（未登录访客无 Add comment 按钮）；私有仓库对无权限者 404。

### REQ-6-3-3 行内评审评论
- 变更行（add/delete）渲染视觉 `+`、可访问名 `Add comment` 的按钮，直接激活无需 hover；文档序第一个按钮=第一个可评论行。
- 打开唯一编辑器 labeled `Comment`（`role=textbox`），按钮 `Add single comment`（立即发布，精确正文，刷新保留，Conversation 同步）与 `Start a review`（pending 草稿，仅作者可见，标 `Pending review`，刷新保留，不公开）。

- 空评论、越界行、非变更行、未知文件均 400 且不产生部分记录；作者与 Read 用户 403。
- 种子：`bob-reviewer`(write, 非作者)、`Fix search`(可评审 Open PR)、`Review sample change`(pending 场景 Open PR)。

### REQ-6-3-4 提交评审
`Review changes` → 单一表单：可选 `Summary`、radio `Comment`/`Approve`/`Request changes`、按钮 `Submit review`；Approve 无 summary 显示 `Approved`；Request changes+summary 显示 `Changes requested` 与精确 summary；刷新保留；作者与 Draft 不能提交（403/400）；同一评审者对同一 compare commit 新决策替换生效决策、历史记录保留在 Conversation；Request changes 阻塞合并直至改判或 compare 分支新 commit 使其过期；提交同时发布该评审者的 pending 草稿。

- **REQ-6-3-1/6-3-2**：详情页 Conversation/Commits/Files changed 与刷新一致性；`Commit summary`、`Changed files summary`、`src/search.ts` 逐字 + `3 additions, 1 deletions`。

- **REQ-6-3-4 提交评审**：`Review changes` 表单（Summary / Comment / Approve / Request changes / Submit review）；决策替换生效决策并保留历史；作者/Draft 拒绝；Request changes 阻塞合并直至改判或新 commit 过期。

- **REQ-6-5 合并**：唯一 `Create a merge commit` → 确认区条件 → `Confirm merge`；事务内双亲 merge commit（`parent_id`=base head、`merge_parent_id`=compare commit）、前移 base head、PR 记 `Merged` 终态与合并者/时间/结果 commit；保护规则独立强制，缺审批 409 `Review required by branch protection` 且 base/PR 不变；被阻塞 PR 保留禁用按钮并事先展示条件。
- **REQ-6-6 关闭/重开**：立即生效无确认、刷新保持、不动分支、Merged 终态、只读查看者不渲染控件。
- **REQ-6-1 分支保护**（Issue 标题范围 + REQ-6-5 前置）：Settings → Branches 规则 CRUD（`Add branch protection rule` / `Branch name pattern` / 两个复选框 / `Create` / `Save changes` / 摘要文案）与 Checks 区 Admin 的 `test status` 更新。

## 关键设计点
- Open/Draft PR 的 base/compare 跟随分支 head（因此新 commit 会使评论 `Outdated`、评审过期、check 回到 pending）；Closed/Merged 冻结。
- 新种子放在独立 public 仓库 `acme/merge-demo`（保护 main + 成功/拒绝/评审 PR）与 `acme/protect-demo`，`acme/web-app` 既有 4 条 PR 与共享编号不变；schema 增量由 `db.js` 幂等迁移补齐。
